Existing methods typically adopt either flexible attention mechanisms or meta-path constrained anchoring, but in heterogeneous multi-label prediction they often suffer from semantic dilution or coverage constraint. Both issues are further amplified under multi-label supervision. We present a theoretical analysis showing that as heterogeneous neighborhoods expand, the attention mass allocated to task-critical (primary) neighborhoods diminishes, and that meta-path constrained aggregation exhibits a dilemma: too few meta-paths intensify coverage constraint, while too many re-introduce dilution. To resolve this coverage-anchoring conflict, we propose FOCAL: Fusion Of Coverage and Anchoring Learning, with two components: coverage-oriented attention (COA) for flexible, unconstrained heterogeneous context aggregation, and anchoring-oriented attention (AOA) that restricts aggregation to meta-path-induced primary semantics. Our theoretical analysis and experimental results further indicates that FOCAL has a better performance than other state-of-the-art methods.

</details>


### [187] [TEMPO: Scaling Test-time Training for Large Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19295)
*Qingyang Zhang,Xinke Kong,Haitao Wu,Qinghua Hu,Minghao Wu,Baosong Yang,Yu Cheng,Yun Luo,Ganqu Cui,Changqing Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TEMPO是一个测试时训练框架，通过交替进行策略精化和评论家重新校准来解决现有方法中的性能停滞和多样性崩溃问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有测试时训练方法在大型推理模型上存在性能快速停滞问题，缺乏外部校准导致自生成奖励信号漂移，造成性能停滞和多样性崩溃。

Method: 提出TEMPO框架，交替进行：1）在未标注问题上精化策略模型；2）在标注数据集上定期重新校准评论家。通过期望最大化算法形式化这一交替过程。

Result: TEMPO显著提升模型性能：OLMO3-7B在AIME 2024上从33.0%提升到51.1%，Qwen3-14B从42.3%提升到65.8%，同时保持高多样性。

Conclusion: 重新引入评论家重新校准步骤对于测试时训练至关重要，能够收紧证据下界，实现持续改进，避免现有方法的不完整性。

Abstract: Test-time training (TTT) adapts model parameters on unlabeled test instances during inference time, which continuously extends capabilities beyond the reach of offline training. Despite initial gains, existing TTT methods for LRMs plateau quickly and do not benefit from additional test-time compute. Without external calibration, the self-generated reward signal increasingly drifts as the policy model evolves, leading to both performance plateaus and diversity collapse. We propose TEMPO, a TTT framework that interleaves policy refinement on unlabeled questions with periodic critic recalibration on a labeled dataset. By formalizing this alternating procedure through the Expectation-Maximization (EM) algorithm, we reveal that prior methods can be interpreted as incomplete variants that omit the crucial recalibration step. Reintroducing this step tightens the evidence lower bound (ELBO) and enables sustained improvement. Across diverse model families (Qwen3 and OLMO3) and reasoning tasks, TEMPO improves OLMO3-7B on AIME 2024 from 33.0% to 51.1% and Qwen3-14B from 42.3% to 65.8%, while maintaining high diversity.

</details>


### [188] [Debiased neural operators for estimating functionals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19296)
*Konstantin Hess,Dennis Frauen,Niki Kilbertus,Stefan Feuerriegel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DOPE是一种半参数估计器，用于从神经算子预测的轨迹中估计标量目标量，通过去偏机制解决朴素估计的一阶偏差问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在许多物理系统应用中，目标不是恢复完整的解轨迹，而是通过标量目标量（如时间阈值、累积成本、总能量等）来总结解轨迹。神经算子虽然能近似解映射，但朴素估计方法存在一阶偏差问题。

Method: 提出DOPE（去偏神经算子）半参数估计器：1) 推导新颖的一步Neyman-正交估计器，将神经算子视为函数空间之间的高维干扰映射；2) 引入加权机制同时处理不规则观测设计和目标量对轨迹扰动的敏感性；3) 通过Riesz回归将自动去偏机器学习扩展到算子值干扰。

Result: DOPE能有效消除朴素估计的一阶偏差，适用于部分和不规则观测设置，可与任意神经算子架构结合，在多个数值实验中表现出优越性能。

Conclusion: DOPE为从神经算子预测的轨迹中估计标量目标量提供了一种有效的去偏估计框架，解决了传统方法的一阶偏差问题，具有广泛的适用性和理论保证。

Abstract: Neural operators are widely used to approximate solution maps of complex physical systems. In many applications, however, the goal is not to recover the full solution trajectory, but to summarize the solution trajectory via a scalar target quantity (e.g., a functional such as time spent in a target range, time above a threshold, accumulated cost, or total energy). In this paper, we introduce DOPE (debiased neural operator): a semiparametric estimator for such target quantities of solution trajectories obtained from neural operators. DOPE is broadly applicable to settings with both partial and irregular observations and can be combined with arbitrary neural operator architectures. We make three main contributions. (1) We show that, in contrast to DOPE, naive plug-in estimation can suffer from first-order bias. (2) To address this, we derive a novel one-step, Neyman-orthogonal estimator that treats the neural operator as a high-dimensional nuisance mapping between function spaces, and removes the leading bias term. For this, DOPE uses a weighting mechanism that simultaneously accounts for irregular observation designs and for how sensitive the target quantity is to perturbations of the underlying trajectory. (3) To learn the weights, we extend automatic debiased machine learning to operator-valued nuisances via Riesz regression. We demonstrate the benefits of DOPE across various numerical experiments.

</details>


### [189] [On the Conditioning Consistency Gap in Conditional Neural Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19312)
*Robin Young*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文分析了条件神经过程（CNP）的Kolmogorov一致性违反问题，定义了"条件一致性间隙"来衡量CNP预测在添加上下文点与条件化时的差异，证明了对于有界编码器和Lipschitz解码器的CNP，该间隙为O(1/n²)且紧致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经过程（NPs）作为元学习模型，虽然受到随机过程的启发，但通常不满足Kolmogorov一致性条件。这种不一致性被广泛认知但理解不足。实践中NPs表现良好，但缺乏对这种违反程度的量化分析。本文旨在填补这一空白，精确理解CNPs在何种意义上近似有效的随机过程。

Method: 定义了"条件一致性间隙"作为KL散度，衡量CNP在添加点到上下文与条件化时的预测变化。对具有有界编码器和Lipschitz解码器的CNPs进行了理论分析，推导一致性间隙的上下界。

Result: 主要结果表明：对于有界编码器和Lipschitz解码器的CNPs，一致性间隙为O(1/n²)，其中n为上下文大小，且该速率是紧致的。这些界限确立了CNPs近似有效随机过程的精确意义。

Conclusion: CNPs的不一致性在中等上下文规模下可以忽略，但在少样本情况下可能显著。该研究为理解神经过程的一致性违反提供了量化框架，揭示了CNPs作为随机过程近似的理论性质。

Abstract: Neural processes are meta-learning models that map context sets to predictive distributions. While inspired by stochastic processes, NPs do not generally satisfy the Kolmogorov consistency conditions required to define a valid stochastic process. This inconsistency is widely acknowledged but poorly understood. Practitioners note that NPs work well despite the violation, without quantifying what this means. We address this gap by defining the conditioning consistency gap, a KL divergence measuring how much a conditional neural process's (CNP) predictions change when a point is added to the context versus conditioned upon. Our main results show that for CNPs with bounded encoders and Lipschitz decoders, the consistency gap is $O(1/n^2)$ in context size $n$, and that this rate is tight. These bounds establish the precise sense in which CNPs approximate valid stochastic processes. The inconsistency is negligible for moderate context sizes but can be significant in the few-shot regime.

</details>


### [190] [RDP LoRA: Geometry-Driven Identification for Parameter-Efficient Adaptation in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19321)
*Yusuf Çelebi,Yağız Asker,Özay Ezerceli,Mahmoud ElHussieni,Selva Taş,Reyhan Bayraktar,Fatma Betül Terzioğlu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于Ramer-Douglas-Peucker算法的几何轨迹分析方法，用于识别大语言模型隐藏状态演化中的关键转折点，并以此指导LoRA微调中的层选择策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管存在LoRA等参数高效微调方法，但大语言模型内部表征的层特异性作用仍不明确，导致层选择决策依赖启发式方法。需要更系统的方法来理解隐藏状态的演化并指导微调。

Method: 将隐藏状态演化建模为高维几何轨迹，应用Ramer-Douglas-Peucker算法识别关键转折点（几何枢轴）。将这些几何枢轴作为决策信号，确定在参数高效微调中应适配的层。在Qwen3-8B-Base上集成此几何感知层选择策略到LoRA微调中。

Result: 在MMLU-Math上，仅使用13个RDP选择层（81.67%）显著优于完整36层适配（79.32%）、随机13层选择（75.56%）和基线模型（74.25%）。几何感知层选择策略在减少计算成本的同时提升了性能。

Conclusion: 利用表征轨迹的内在几何特性为模型适配中的层选择提供了鲁棒、可解释且无需训练的信号，优化了参数高效微调的效果。

Abstract: Fine-tuning Large Language Models (LLMs) remains structurally uncertain despite parameter-efficient methods such as Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA), as the layer-specific roles of internal representations are poorly understood, leading to heuristic decisions about where adaptation should be applied. We model the evolution of hidden states as a high-dimensional geometric trajectory and propose using the Ramer-Douglas-Peucker (RDP) algorithm, a parameter-free and training-free polygon simplification method that preserves global structural transitions while eliminating locally redundant changes, to identify critical breakpoints along the representation path. Crucially, we use these geometric pivots not merely for analysis, but as a direct decision signal for determining which layers should be adapted during parameter-efficient fine-tuning. By integrating this geometry-aware layer selection strategy into LoRA fine-tuning of Qwen3-8B-Base, we achieve superior performance on MMLU-Math using only 13 RDP-selected layers (81.67%), significantly outperforming both full 36-layer adaptation (79.32%) and random 13-layer selection (75.56%), as well as the baseline Qwen3-8B-Base model (74.25%). These results demonstrate that leveraging the intrinsic geometry of representation trajectories provides a robust, interpretable, and training-free signal for optimizing layer selection during model adaptation.

</details>


### [191] [Concept Inconsistency in Dermoscopic Concept Bottleneck Models: A Rough-Set Analysis of the Derm7pt Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19323)
*Gonzalo Nápoles,Isel Grau,Yamisleydi Salgueiro*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究应用粗糙集理论分析Derm7pt皮肤镜数据集，发现概念瓶颈模型(CBMs)因概念层不一致性存在92.1%的理论准确率上限，提出过滤策略创建完全一致的Derm7pt+子集，并在19个骨干架构上建立可复现的CBM基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 概念瓶颈模型(CBMs)通过临床概念层进行预测，但当数据集中存在概念级不一致时（相同概念配置对应冲突的诊断标签），会形成无法解决的瓶颈，对可达到的准确率设置硬性上限。本研究旨在量化这种不一致性的程度和临床结构。

Method: 应用粗糙集理论分析Derm7pt皮肤镜基准数据集（7点黑色素瘤检查表的7个皮肤镜标准）。识别不一致概念配置，量化冲突严重性分布，确定导致边界模糊的临床特征。评估两种过滤策略（对称和不对称过滤）对数据集组成和CBM可解释性的影响。

Result: 在305个独特概念配置中，50个（16.4%）存在不一致，涉及306张图像（占数据集的30.3%），导致理论准确率上限为92.1%。对称过滤创建了Derm7pt+子集（705张图像），完全一致且无硬性准确率上限。在19个骨干架构评估中，对称过滤下EfficientNet-B5获得最佳标签F1分数（0.85）和标签准确率（0.90）；不对称过滤下EfficientNet-B7表现最佳（标签F1分数0.82）。

Conclusion: 概念不一致性是CBMs在皮肤镜数据上的重要限制因素，通过粗糙集分析可以量化并缓解这一问题。提出的过滤策略创建了完全一致的数据集子集，为概念一致的CBM评估建立了可复现的基准。这些发现强调了在医学AI中确保概念-标签一致性的重要性。

Abstract: Concept Bottleneck Models (CBMs) route predictions exclusively through a clinically grounded concept layer, binding interpretability to concept-label consistency. When a dataset contains concept-level inconsistencies, identical concept profiles mapped to conflicting diagnosis labels create an unresolvable bottleneck that imposes a hard ceiling on achievable accuracy. In this paper, we apply rough set theory to the Derm7pt dermoscopy benchmark and characterize the full extent and clinical structure of this inconsistency. Among 305 unique concept profiles formed by the 7 dermoscopic criteria of the 7-point melanoma checklist, 50 (16.4%) are inconsistent, spanning 306 images (30.3% of the dataset). This yields a theoretical accuracy ceiling of 92.1%, independent of backbone architecture or training strategy for CBMs that exclusively operate with hard concepts. In addition, we characterize the conflict-severity distribution, identify the clinical features most responsible for boundary ambiguity, and evaluate two filtering strategies with quantified effects on dataset composition and CBM interpretability. Symmetric removal of all boundary-region images yields Derm7pt+, a fully consistent benchmark subset of 705 images with perfect quality of classification and no hard accuracy ceiling. Building on this filtered dataset, we present a hard CBM evaluated across 19 backbone architectures from the EfficientNet, DenseNet, ResNet, and Wide ResNet families. Under symmetric filtering, explored for completeness, EfficientNet-B5 achieves the best label F1 score (0.85) and label accuracy (0.90) on the held-out test set, with a concept accuracy of 0.70. Under asymmetric filtering, EfficientNet-B7 leads across all four metrics, reaching a label F1 score of 0.82 and concept accuracy of 0.70. These results establish reproducible baselines for concept-consistent CBM evaluation on dermoscopic data.

</details>


### [192] [When Active Learning Falls Short: An Empirical Study on Chemical Reaction Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19335)
*Simin Yu,Sufia Fathima*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了主动学习在化学反应提取中的应用，评估了六种不确定性/多样性策略与预训练transformer-CRF架构的结合效果，发现预训练强度、CRF解码结构和标签稀疏性限制了传统主动学习策略的稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 化学文献快速增长产生了大量非结构化数据，其中反应信息对反应预测和药物设计等应用特别有价值。然而专家标注成本过高导致训练数据稀缺，严重阻碍了自动反应提取的性能。

Method: 整合六种基于不确定性和多样性的主动学习策略与预训练transformer-CRF架构，在产物提取和角色标注任务上进行评估。

Result: 多种方法在较少标注实例下接近全数据性能，但学习曲线通常是非单调且任务依赖的。分析表明强预训练、结构化CRF解码和标签稀疏性限制了传统主动学习策略的稳定性。

Conclusion: 这些发现为在化学信息提取中有效使用主动学习提供了实用见解，强调了考虑预训练强度、模型架构和标签分布对主动学习策略稳定性的影响。

Abstract: The rapid growth of chemical literature has generated vast amounts of unstructured data, where reaction information is particularly valuable for applications such as reaction predictions and drug design. However, the prohibitive cost of expert annotation has led to a scarcity of training data, severely hindering the performance of automatic reaction extraction. In this work, we conduct a systematic study of active learning for chemical reaction extraction. We integrate six uncertainty- and diversity-based strategies with pretrained transformer-CRF architectures, and evaluate them on product extraction and role labeling task. While several methods approach full-data performance with fewer labeled instances, learning curves are often non-monotonic and task-dependent. Our analysis shows that strong pretraining, structured CRF decoding, and label sparsity limit the stability of conventional active learning strategies. These findings provide practical insights for the effective use of active learning in chemical information extraction.

</details>


### [193] [Evaluation-driven Scaling for Scientific Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19341)
*Haotian Ye,Haowei Lin,Jingyi Tang,Yizhen Luo,Caiyin Yang,Chang Su,Rahul Thapa,Rui Yang,Ruihua Liu,Zeyu Li,Chong Gao,Dachao Ding,Guangrong He,Miaolei Zhang,Lina Sun,Wenyang Wang,Yuchen Zhong,Zhuohao Shen,Di He,Jianzhu Ma,Stefano Ermon,Tongyang Li,Xiaowen Chu,James Zou,Yuzhi Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SimpleTES框架通过并行探索、反馈驱动优化和局部选择，系统化扩展评估驱动的科学发现循环，在多个领域实现突破性成果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究虽然认识到评估在科学发现中的重要性，但缺乏系统化扩展评估驱动发现循环的原则性方法，限制了语言模型在科学发现中的潜力。

Method: 提出SimpleTES框架，结合并行探索、反馈驱动优化和局部选择三个核心组件，系统化扩展评估驱动的发现循环，并利用轨迹历史监督反馈驱动的学习。

Result: 在6个领域的21个科学问题上，SimpleTES使用开源GPT模型发现了最先进的解决方案，显著优于前沿模型基线和复杂优化流程。具体成果包括：加速LASSO算法2倍以上，设计减少24.5%门开销的量子电路路由策略，发现超越最佳已知结果的Erdos最小重叠构造。

Conclusion: 有效的评估驱动循环扩展是推进LLM驱动科学发现的核心轴，SimpleTES提供了一个简单实用的框架来实现这些收益，同时轨迹历史监督的反馈驱动学习使模型能够泛化到未见问题。

Abstract: Language models are increasingly used in scientific discovery to generate hypotheses, propose candidate solutions, implement systems, and iteratively refine them. At the core of these trial-and-error loops lies evaluation: the process of obtaining feedback on candidate solutions via verifiers, simulators, or task-specific scoring functions. While prior work has highlighted the importance of evaluation, it has not explicitly formulated the problem of how evaluation-driven discovery loops can be scaled up in a principled and effective manner to push the boundaries of scientific discovery, a problem this paper seeks to address. We introduce Simple Test-time Evaluation-driven Scaling (SimpleTES), a general framework that strategically combines parallel exploration, feedback-driven refinement, and local selection, revealing substantial gains unlocked by scaling evaluation-driven discovery loops along the right dimensions. Across 21 scientific problems spanning six domains, SimpleTES discovers state-of-the-art solutions using gpt-oss models, consistently outperforming both frontier-model baselines and sophisticated optimization pipelines. Particularly, we sped up the widely used LASSO algorithm by over 2x, designed quantum circuit routing policies that reduce gate overhead by 24.5%, and discovered new Erdos minimum overlap constructions that surpass the best-known results. Beyond novel discoveries, SimpleTES produces trajectory-level histories that naturally supervise feedback-driven learning. When post-trained on successful trajectories, models not only improve efficiency on seen problems but also generalize to unseen problems, discovering solutions that base models fail to uncover. Together, our results establish effective evaluation-driven loop scaling as a central axis for advancing LLM-driven scientific discovery, and provide a simple yet practical framework for realizing these gains.

</details>


### [194] [LASER: Learning Active Sensing for Continuum Field Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19355)
*Huayu Deng,Jinghui Zhong,Xiangming Zhu,Yunbo Wang,Xiaokang Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LASER：一种基于强化学习的主动感知框架，通过POMDP建模和连续场潜在世界模型，实现传感器布局的动态优化以适应物理场演化


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续物理场的高保真测量对科学发现和工程设计至关重要，但在稀疏和受限感知条件下仍具挑战。传统重建方法通常依赖固定传感器布局，无法适应演化的物理状态。

Method: 提出LASER统一闭环框架，将主动感知建模为部分可观测马尔可夫决策过程（POMDP）。核心是连续场潜在世界模型，捕捉底层物理动力学并提供内在奖励反馈。强化学习策略在潜在想象空间中模拟"假设"感知场景，通过预测潜在状态调节传感器移动，导航至高信息区域。

Result: 实验表明，LASER在多种连续场中一致优于静态和离线优化策略，在稀疏条件下实现高保真重建。

Conclusion: LASER通过闭环主动感知框架，实现了传感器布局的动态适应和优化，为稀疏感知条件下的连续物理场高保真测量提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: High-fidelity measurements of continuum physical fields are essential for scientific discovery and engineering design but remain challenging under sparse and constrained sensing. Conventional reconstruction methods typically rely on fixed sensor layouts, which cannot adapt to evolving physical states. We propose LASER, a unified, closed-loop framework that formulates active sensing as a Partially Observable Markov Decision Process (POMDP). At its core, LASER employs a continuum field latent world model that captures the underlying physical dynamics and provides intrinsic reward feedback. This enables a reinforcement learning policy to simulate ''what-if'' sensing scenarios within a latent imagination space. By conditioning sensor movements on predicted latent states, LASER navigates toward potentially high-information regions beyond current observations. Our experiments demonstrate that LASER consistently outperforms static and offline-optimized strategies, achieving high-fidelity reconstruction under sparsity across diverse continuum fields.

</details>


### [195] [FairTree: Subgroup Fairness Auditing of Machine Learning Models with Bias-Variance Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19357)
*Rudolf Debelak*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FairTree是一种基于心理测量不变性测试的新算法，用于检测机器学习模型在不同子组中的性能差异，可直接处理连续、分类和有序特征，无需离散化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前机器学习模型评估主要依赖基于损失函数的性能指标，可能忽略相关子组中的性能变化。现有审计工具如SliceFinder和SliceLine存在概念缺陷，如无法直接处理连续协变量。

Method: 提出FairTree算法，基于心理测量不变性测试，可直接处理连续、分类和有序特征。算法将性能差异分解为系统偏差和方差，并提出两种变体：基于置换的方法（概念上更接近SliceFinder）和波动测试。

Result: 通过模拟研究（包括与SliceLine的直接比较）表明，两种方法都有令人满意的假阳性率，但波动测试方法具有相对更高的统计功效。在UCI成人人口普查数据集上进行了方法演示。

Conclusion: FairTree算法为统计评估机器学习模型的性能和公平性方面提供了一个灵活框架，即使在相对较小的数据集中也能广泛应用。

Abstract: The evaluation of machine learning models typically relies mainly on performance metrics based on loss functions, which risk to overlook changes in performance in relevant subgroups. Auditing tools such as SliceFinder and SliceLine were proposed to detect such groups, but usually have conceptual disadvantages, such as the inability to directly address continuous covariates. In this paper, we introduce FairTree, a novel algorithm adapted from psychometric invariance testing. Unlike SliceFinder and related algorithms, FairTree directly handles continuous, categorical, and ordinal features without discretization. It further decomposes performance disparities into systematic bias and variance, allowing a categorization of changes in algorithm performance. We propose and evaluate two variations of the algorithm: a permutation-based approach, which is conceptually closer to SliceFinder, and a fluctuation test. Through simulation studies that include a direct comparison with SliceLine, we demonstrate that both approaches have a satisfactory rate of false-positive results, but that the fluctuation approach has relatively higher power. We further illustrate the method on the UCI Adult Census dataset. The proposed algorithms provide a flexible framework for the statistical evaluation of the performance and aspects of fairness of machine learning models in a wide range of applications even in relatively small data.

</details>


### [196] [TACENR: Task-Agnostic Contrastive Explanations for Node Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19372)
*Vasiliki Papanikou,Evaggelia Pitoura*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TACENR是一种任务无关的对比解释方法，用于解释节点表示，能够识别属性、邻近性和结构特征的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图表示学习方法生成的节点表示不透明且难以解释，现有解释方法主要关注监督设置或解释单个维度，缺乏对节点表示整体结构的解释。

Method: 提出TACENR方法，基于对比学习学习表示空间中的相似性函数，识别对节点表示最重要的属性特征、邻近性特征和结构特征。

Result: 实验结果表明邻近性和结构特征在塑造节点表示中起重要作用，监督变体在识别最有影响特征方面与现有任务特定方法表现相当。

Conclusion: TACENR填补了图表示解释的关键空白，提供任务无关的局部解释，能够揭示节点表示中各种特征的重要性。

Abstract: Graph representation learning has achieved notable success in encoding graph-structured data into latent vector spaces, enabling a wide range of downstream tasks. However, these node representations remain opaque and difficult to interpret. Existing explainability methods primarily focus on supervised settings or on explaining individual representation dimensions, leaving a critical gap in explaining the overall structure of node representations. In this paper, we propose TACENR (Task-Agnostic Contrastive Explanations for Node Representations), a local explanation method that identifies not only attribute features but also proximity and structural ones that contribute the most in the representation space. TACENR builds on contrastive learning, through which we learn a similarity function in the representation space, revealing which are the features that play an important role in the representation of a node. While our focus is on task-agnostic explanations, TACENR can be applied to supervised scenarios as well. Experimental results demonstrate that proximity and structural features play a significant role in shaping node representations and that our supervised variant performs comparably to existing task-specific approaches in identifying the most impactful features.

</details>


### [197] [Revisiting Catastrophic Forgetting in Continual Knowledge Graph Embedding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19401)
*Gerard Pons,Carlos Escolano,Besim Bilalli,Anna Queralt*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文指出当前持续知识图谱嵌入方法忽略了新实体引入时产生的实体干扰问题，导致对灾难性遗忘的评估存在误导性，并提出了修正的评估协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前持续知识图谱嵌入方法主要关注限制现有嵌入的变化来应对灾难性遗忘，但忽略了新实体引入时产生的实体干扰问题。这种干扰会导致模型将新实体误预测为先前正确答案，而现有评估协议未能考虑这一现象，导致对CKGE方法性能的系统性高估。

Method: 1. 识别并定义了"实体干扰"现象，即新实体嵌入与先前学习嵌入之间的干扰问题；2. 提出了修正的CKGE评估协议，专门考虑实体干扰效应；3. 在多个基准测试上进行实验验证；4. 分析了不同CKGE方法和KGE模型受不同遗忘来源的影响；5. 引入了针对CKGE定制的灾难性遗忘度量指标。

Result: 实验表明，忽略实体干扰效应会导致性能高估高达25%，特别是在实体显著增长的场景中。研究揭示了现有评估协议对灾难性遗忘评估的误导性，并展示了不同CKGE方法和模型受实体干扰影响的程度差异。

Conclusion: 实体干扰是持续知识图谱嵌入中一个被忽视但重要的问题，现有评估协议需要修正以准确反映CKGE方法的真实性能。提出的修正评估协议和定制化遗忘度量指标为更准确的CKGE评估提供了基础，强调了在持续学习环境中考虑实体间交互的重要性。

Abstract: Knowledge Graph Embeddings (KGEs) support a wide range of downstream tasks over Knowledge Graphs (KGs). In practice, KGs evolve as new entities and facts are added, motivating Continual Knowledge Graph Embedding (CKGE) methods that update embeddings over time. Current CKGE approaches address catastrophic forgetting (i.e., the performance degradation on previously learned tasks) primarily by limiting changes to existing embeddings.
  However, we show that this view is incomplete. When new entities are introduced, their embeddings can interfere with previously learned ones, causing the model to predict them in place of previously correct answers. This phenomenon, which we call entity interference, has been largely overlooked and is not accounted for in current CKGE evaluation protocols. As a result, the assessment of catastrophic forgetting becomes misleading, and CKGE methods performance is systematically overestimated.
  To address this issue, we introduce a corrected CKGE evaluation protocol that accounts for entity interference. Through experiments on multiple benchmarks, we show that ignoring this effect can lead to performance overestimation of up to 25%, particularly in scenarios with significant entity growth. We further analyze how different CKGE methods and KGE models are affected by the different sources of forgetting, and introduce a catastrophic forgetting metric tailored to CKGE.

</details>


### [198] [Heterogeneity-Aware Personalized Federated Learning for Industrial Predictive Analytics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19451)
*Yuhan Hu,Xiaolei Fang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出个性化联邦预测模型，解决传统联邦学习中退化过程同质性假设不成立的问题，通过相似退化模式客户端间的成对协作提升个性化联邦学习性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统联邦预测模型假设各客户端的退化过程具有同质性，但在实际工业场景中，不同公司、工厂或生产线的退化过程往往是异质的。现有模型无法有效处理这种异质性，需要开发能够适应异质退化过程的个性化联邦预测方法。

Method: 提出个性化联邦预测模型，通过迭代促进具有相似退化模式的客户端间成对协作。开发基于近端梯度下降的联邦参数估计算法，使用分散数据集联合估计参数。模型同时实现个性化、数据隐私保护和完整故障时间分布预测。

Result: 通过广泛的仿真研究和NASA涡轮风扇发动机退化数据集的案例研究验证了所提模型的优越性。模型在保持数据隐私的同时，能够为异质客户端构建定制化的预测模型，并提供全面的故障时间分布。

Conclusion: 所提出的个性化联邦预测模型克服了现有联邦预测模型的局限性，能够有效处理客户端间的异质退化过程，在保持数据隐私的同时实现模型个性化，为工业预测维护提供了更实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Federated prognostics enable clients (e.g., companies, factories, and production lines) to collaboratively develop a failure time prediction model while keeping each client's data local and confidential. However, traditional federated models often assume homogeneity in the degradation processes across clients, an assumption that may not hold in many industrial settings. To overcome this, this paper proposes a personalized federated prognostic model designed to accommodate clients with heterogeneous degradation processes, allowing them to build tailored prognostic models. The prognostic model iteratively facilitates the underlying pairwise collaborations between clients with similar degradation patterns, which enhances the performance of personalized federated learning. To estimate parameters jointly using decentralized datasets, we develop a federated parameter estimation algorithm based on proximal gradient descent. The proposed approach addresses the limitations of existing federated prognostic models by simultaneously achieving model personalization, preserving data privacy, and providing comprehensive failure time distributions. The superiority of the proposed model is validated through extensive simulation studies and a case study using the turbofan engine degradation dataset from the NASA repository.

</details>


### [199] [EVPO: Explained Variance Policy Optimization for Adaptive Critic Utilization in LLM Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19485)
*Chengjun Pan,Shichun Liu,Jiahang Lin,Dingwei Zhu,Jiazheng Zhang,Shihan Dou,Songyang Gao,Zhenhua Han,Binghai Wang,Rui Zheng,Xuanjing Huang,Tao Gui,Yansong Feng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: EVPO提出基于解释方差的自适应基线选择方法，在稀疏奖励场景中动态切换基于评论家的优势估计和批均值估计，优于固定使用PPO或GRPO。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习用于LLM后训练时面临一个基本设计选择：是否使用学习的评论家作为策略优化的基线。经典理论支持基于评论家的方法（如PPO）以降低方差，但无评论家方法（如GRPO）因其简单性和竞争性表现而广泛采用。作者发现在稀疏奖励设置中，学习的评论家可能引入超过其捕获状态信号的估计噪声，反而增加优势方差。

Method: 将基线选择建模为卡尔曼滤波问题，将PPO和GRPO统一为卡尔曼增益的两个极端。证明解释方差（EV）可从单个训练批次计算，并识别出精确边界：正EV表示评论家降低方差，零或负EV表示评论家增加方差。基于此提出解释方差策略优化（EVPO），在每一步训练中监控批次级EV，自适应地在基于评论家的优势估计和批均值估计之间切换，理论上保证每一步的方差不大于两者中较好的那个。

Result: 在涵盖经典控制、智能体交互和数学推理的四个任务中，EVPO始终优于PPO和GRPO，无论哪个固定基线在特定任务上更强。进一步分析确认自适应门控能跟踪评论家在训练过程中的成熟度，且理论推导的零阈值在经验上是最优的。

Conclusion: EVPO通过基于解释方差的自适应基线选择，解决了稀疏奖励场景中评论家可能增加方差的问题，提供了一种理论上合理且经验有效的策略优化方法，优于现有的固定基线方法。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) for LLM post-training faces a fundamental design choice: whether to use a learned critic as a baseline for policy optimization. Classical theory favors critic-based methods such as PPO for variance reduction, yet critic-free alternatives like GRPO have gained widespread adoption due to their simplicity and competitive performance. We show that in sparse-reward settings, a learned critic can inject estimation noise that exceeds the state signal it captures, increasing rather than reducing advantage variance. By casting baseline selection as a Kalman filtering problem, we unify PPO and GRPO as two extremes of the Kalman gain and prove that explained variance (EV), computable from a single training batch, identifies the exact boundary: positive EV indicates the critic reduces variance, while zero or negative EV signals that it inflates variance. Building on this insight, we propose Explained Variance Policy Optimization (EVPO), which monitors batch-level EV at each training step and adaptively switches between critic-based and batch-mean advantage estimation, provably achieving no greater variance than the better of the two at every step. Across four tasks spanning classical control, agentic interaction, and mathematical reasoning, EVPO consistently outperforms both PPO and GRPO regardless of which fixed baseline is stronger on a given task. Further analysis confirms that the adaptive gating tracks critic maturation over training and that the theoretically derived zero threshold is empirically optimal.

</details>


### [200] [When Graph Structure Becomes a Liability: A Critical Re-Evaluation of Graph Neural Networks for Bitcoin Fraud Detection under Temporal Distribution Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19514)
*Saket Maganti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文挑战了GNN在Elliptic比特币数据集上优于特征基线方法的共识，通过严格的防泄漏评估协议发现，在严格归纳设置下，随机森林等特征方法显著优于所有GNN模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究普遍认为GCN、GraphSAGE、GAT和EvolveGCN在Elliptic比特币数据集上优于仅使用特征的基线方法，但这种共识缺乏在防泄漏评估协议下的严格验证。作者旨在通过种子匹配的归纳与转导对比，检验这一共识是否成立。

Method: 采用种子匹配的归纳与转导对比实验，实施严格的归纳协议防止训练时暴露测试期邻接信息。进行配对控制实验量化邻接信息泄漏的影响，通过边洗牌消融实验分析图拓扑的有效性，并评估结合GNN嵌入与原始特征的混合模型。

Result: 在严格归纳协议下，随机森林在原始特征上达到F1=0.821，优于所有评估的GNN（GraphSAGE仅达F1=0.689±0.017）。配对实验显示39.5分的F1差距源于训练时暴露测试期邻接信息。边洗牌实验表明随机连通的图优于真实交易图，表明在时间分布偏移下数据集拓扑具有误导性。混合模型仅提供边际增益且仍显著低于特征基线。

Conclusion: 现有关于GNN在Elliptic比特币数据集上优于特征基线的共识在严格防泄漏评估下不成立。数据集拓扑在时间分布偏移下具有误导性，未来研究应采用严格的归纳协议进行可重复、无泄漏的评估。作者发布了代码、检查点和严格归纳协议以促进可重复研究。

Abstract: The consensus that GCN, GraphSAGE, GAT, and EvolveGCN outperform feature-only baselines on the Elliptic Bitcoin Dataset is widely cited but has not been rigorously stress-tested under a leakage-free evaluation protocol. We perform a seed-matched inductive-versus-transductive comparison and find that this consensus does not hold. Under a strictly inductive protocol, Random Forest on raw features achieves F1 = 0.821 and outperforms all evaluated GNNs, while GraphSAGE reaches F1 = 0.689 +/- 0.017. A paired controlled experiment reveals a 39.5-point F1 gap attributable to training-time exposure to test-period adjacency. Additionally, edge-shuffle ablations show that randomly wired graphs outperform the real transaction graph, indicating that the dataset's topology can be misleading under temporal distribution shift. Hybrid models combining GNN embeddings with raw features provide only marginal gains and remain substantially below feature-only baselines. We release code, checkpoints, and a strict-inductive protocol to enable reproducible, leakage-free evaluation.

</details>


### [201] [Revisiting RaBitQ and TurboQuant: A Symmetric Comparison of Methods, Theory, and Experiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19528)
*Jianyang Gao,Yutong Gou,Yuexuan Xu,Jifan Shi,Yongyi Yang,Shuolin Li,Raymond Chi-Wing Wong,Cheng Long*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 对RaBitQ和TurboQuant两种量化方法在统一框架下进行对比分析，发现TurboQuant并未提供一致的改进，在许多配置下表现更差，且原论文中的部分实验结果无法复现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重新审视RaBitQ和TurboQuant两种量化方法的关系，在统一、可复现、透明且对称的实验设置下进行公平比较，澄清两种方法的真实差异，并验证TurboQuant论文中声称的优势是否成立。

Method: 建立统一的比较框架，从方法论、理论保证和实证性能三个维度对比两种方法。采用可复现、透明且对称的实验设置，确保比较的公平性。对TurboQuant论文中报告的运行时和召回率结果进行复现验证。

Result: 尽管TurboQuant声称有优势，但在直接可比的设置下并未提供对RaBitQ的一致改进；在许多测试配置中，TurboQuant表现比RaBitQ更差。此外，TurboQuant论文中报告的多个运行时和召回率结果无法从其发布的实现中复现。

Conclusion: 本技术说明澄清了两种方法共享的结构和真实差异，同时记录了TurboQuant论文中实验结果的可复现性问题。研究结果表明需要更严谨的评估标准来比较量化方法。

Abstract: This technical note revisits the relationship between RaBitQ and TurboQuant under a unified comparison framework. We compare the two methods in terms of methodology, theoretical guarantees, and empirical performance, using a reproducible, transparent, and symmetric setup. Our results show that, despite the claimed advantage of TurboQuant, TurboQuant does not provide a consistent improvement over RaBitQ in directly comparable settings; in many tested configurations, it performs worse than RaBitQ. We further find that several reported runtime and recall results in the TurboQuant paper could not be reproduced from the released implementation under the stated configuration. Overall, this note clarifies the shared structure and genuine differences between the two lines of work, while documenting reproducibility issues in the experimental results reported by the TurboQuant paper.

</details>


### [202] [Calibrating Scientific Foundation Models with Inference-Time Stochastic Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19530)
*Akash Yadav,Taiwo A. Adebiyi,Ruda Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Stochastic Attention方法，通过随机化注意力权重实现预测不确定性校准，无需重新训练即可生成预测集成


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer基础模型在科学应用中缺乏校准的预测不确定性支持，当前架构输出确定性结果且不确定性校准有限

Method: 提出随机注意力机制，用归一化多项式样本替换softmax权重，通过单一浓度参数控制随机化；引入校准目标匹配随机注意力输出与目标，形成高效单变量后验调优问题

Result: 在天气和时间序列预测等科学基础模型上，随机注意力相比不确定性感知基线获得最强的原生校准和最尖锐的预测区间，同时后验调优仅需分钟级时间而非基线所需的数天重新训练

Conclusion: 随机注意力为Transformer基础模型提供轻量级推理时修改，实现校准的预测不确定性，在保持覆盖率的条件下提供更尖锐的预测区间，显著降低计算成本

Abstract: Transformer-based scientific foundation models are increasingly deployed in high-stakes settings, but current architectures give deterministic outputs and provide limited support for calibrated predictive uncertainty. We propose Stochastic Attention, a lightweight inference-time modification that randomizes attention by replacing softmax weights with normalized multinomial samples controlled by a single concentration parameter, and produces predictive ensembles without retraining. To set this parameter, we introduce a calibration objective that matches the stochastic attention output with the target, yielding an efficient univariate post-hoc tuning problem. We evaluate this mechanism on two scientific foundation models for weather and timeseries forecasting along with an additional regression task. Across benchmarks against uncertainty-aware baselines, we find that Stochastic Attention achieves the strongest native calibration and the sharpest prediction intervals at comparable coverage, while requiring only minutes of post-hoc tuning versus days of retraining for competitive baselines.

</details>


### [203] [Separating Geometry from Probability in the Analysis of Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19560)
*Maxim Raginsky,Benjamin Recht*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种基于优化问题敏感性分析的确定性泛化分析框架，替代传统的概率统计假设


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统机器学习泛化分析依赖于无法验证的i.i.d.概率假设，需要一种不依赖统计假设的确定性分析框架

Method: 通过优化问题解对问题数据扰动的敏感性分析，建立变分原理将样本内和样本外评估联系起来

Result: 获得了纯确定性泛化界，通过误差项量化样本外数据与样本内数据的接近程度，统计假设可后验使用

Conclusion: 提供了一种不依赖先验概率假设的泛化分析新视角，统计假设可作为后验工具而非先验条件

Abstract: The goal of machine learning is to find models that minimize prediction error on data that has not yet been seen. Its operational paradigm assumes access to a dataset $S$ and articulates a scheme for evaluating how well a given model performs on an arbitrary sample. The sample can be $S$ (in which case we speak of ``in-sample'' performance) or some entirely new $S'$ (in which case we speak of ``out-of-sample'' performance). Traditional analysis of generalization assumes that both in- and out-of-sample data are i.i.d.\ draws from an infinite population. However, these probabilistic assumptions cannot be verified even in principle. This paper presents an alternative view of generalization through the lens of sensitivity analysis of solutions of optimization problems to perturbations in the problem data. Under this framework, generalization bounds are obtained by purely deterministic means and take the form of variational principles that relate in-sample and out-of-sample evaluations through an error term that quantifies how close out-of-sample data are to in-sample data. Statistical assumptions can then be used \textit{ex post} to characterize the situations when this error term is small (either on average or with high probability).

</details>


### [204] [Structure-guided molecular design with contrastive 3D protein-ligand learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19562)
*Carles Navarro,Philipp Tholke,Gianni de Fabritiis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出统一框架，结合对比3D结构编码与基于商业化合物空间的自回归分子生成，解决结构药物发现中的准确捕获3D相互作用与导航超大化学空间的双重挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结构药物发现面临双重挑战：需要准确捕获3D蛋白-配体相互作用，同时需要在超大化学空间中导航以识别可合成的候选分子。现有方法难以同时满足这两个要求。

Method: 1. 引入SE(3)-等变transformer，通过对比学习将配体和口袋结构编码到共享嵌入空间；2. 将这些嵌入集成到多模态化学语言模型(MCLM)中，该模型基于口袋或配体结构生成靶点特异性分子，并利用学习的数据集标记引导输出到目标化学空间。

Result: 1. SE(3)-等变transformer在零样本虚拟筛选中取得竞争性结果；2. MCLM模型生成的候选分子在多种靶点上展现出有利的预测结合特性。

Conclusion: 该统一框架成功解决了结构药物发现中的双重挑战，通过结合对比3D结构编码和条件分子生成，能够产生具有良好预测结合特性的靶点特异性候选分子。

Abstract: Structure-based drug discovery faces the dual challenge of accurately capturing 3D protein-ligand interactions while navigating ultra-large chemical spaces to identify synthetically accessible candidates. In this work, we present a unified framework that addresses these challenges by combining contrastive 3D structure encoding with autoregressive molecular generation conditioned on commercial compound spaces. First, we introduce an SE(3)-equivariant transformer that encodes ligand and pocket structures into a shared embedding space via contrastive learning, achieving competitive results in zero-shot virtual screening. Second, we integrate these embeddings into a multimodal Chemical Language Model (MCLM). The model generates target-specific molecules conditioned on either pocket or ligand structures, with a learned dataset token that steers the output toward targeted chemical spaces, yielding candidates with favorable predicted binding properties across diverse targets.

</details>


### [205] [An Efficient Black-Box Reduction from Online Learning to Multicalibration, and a New Route to $Φ$-Regret Minimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19592)
*Gabriele Farina,Juan Carlos Perdomo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了从在线学习到在线多校准的GGM风格黑盒约简，建立了多校准与期望变分不等式求解的等价关系，统一了现有算法分析并扩展了应用场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决Garg等人(SODA '24)提出的主要开放问题：证明具有√T型保证的预言机高效在线多校准在完全一般性下是可能的。统一现有在线多校准算法的分析，并扩展到具有延迟观测或审查结果的挑战性环境。

Method: 1. 提出GGM风格黑盒约简：将任何在函数类H上的无遗憾学习器与期望变分不等式(EVI)求解器结合，实现高维多校准。2. 证明逆命题：高效多校准意味着高效EVI求解。3. 建立从高维在线多校准到(上下文)Φ-遗憾最小化的细粒度约简。

Result: 1. 完全解决了SODA '24的主要开放问题，证明了具有√T型保证的预言机高效在线多校准在完全一般性下是可能的。2. 统一了现有在线多校准算法的分析。3. 实现了在线学习与多类全能预测之间的首个高效黑盒约简。4. 简化了Daskalakis等人(STOC '25)的结果并改进了速率，获得了对更丰富偏差类(如再生核希尔伯特空间)鲁棒的新算法。

Conclusion: 该论文通过建立在线学习与在线多校准之间的GGM风格约简，解决了该领域的核心开放问题，统一了现有理论框架，并开辟了从外部遗憾到Φ-遗憾的新路径，显著简化了先前复杂的结果，同时扩展了算法的适用性和鲁棒性。

Abstract: We give a Gordon-Greenwald-Marks (GGM) style black-box reduction from online learning to online multicalibration. Concretely, we show that to achieve high-dimensional multicalibration with respect to a class of functions H, it suffices to combine any no-regret learner over H with an expected variational inequality (EVI) solver. We also prove a converse statement showing that efficient multicalibration implies efficient EVI solving, highlighting how EVIs in multicalibration mirror the role of fixed points in the GGM result for $Φ$-regret.
  This first set of results resolves the main open question in Garg, Jung, Reingold, and Roth (SODA '24), showing that oracle-efficient online multicalibration with $\sqrt{T}$-type guarantees is possible in full generality. Furthermore, our GGM-style reduction unifies the analyses of existing online multicalibration algorithms, enables new algorithms for challenging environments with delayed observations or censored outcomes, and yields the first efficient black-box reduction between online learning and multiclass omniprediction.
  Our second main result is a fine-grained reduction from high-dimensional online multicalibration to (contextual) $Φ$-regret minimization. Together with our first result, this establishes a new route from external regret to Phi-regret that bypasses sophisticated fixed-point or semi-separation machinery, dramatically simplifies a result of Daskalakis, Farina, Fishelson, Pipis, and Schneider (STOC '25) while improving rates, and yields new algorithms that are robust to richer deviation classes, such as those belonging to any reproducing kernel Hilbert space.

</details>


### [206] [SAGE: Training-Free Semantic Evidence Composition for Edge-Cloud Inference under Hard Uplink Budgets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19623)
*Inhyeok Choi,Hyuncheol Park*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出SAGE方法，在边缘-云混合推理中，通过结合重要性过滤和嵌入多样性采样，在硬比特预算约束下优化传输内容选择，显著优于仅基于注意力的标准方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘-云混合推理中，上行信道对传输比特数有硬性约束。传统基于注意力重要性的内容选择方法在硬预算下存在固有局限性，需要更优的传输内容选择策略。

Method: 提出SAGE（语义注意力引导证据）方法，这是一种无需训练的方法，结合重要性过滤和嵌入多样性采样。首先基于注意力重要性筛选候选单元，然后通过嵌入空间中的多样性采样选择最终传输集，确保覆盖输入的不同语义方面。

Result: 在ImageNet-1K上，SAGE仅传输不到一半可用证据单元就达到服务器上限精度的93%，显著优于仅基于重要性的组合方法。实验证明空间覆盖本身具有独立价值。

Conclusion: 在硬预算约束下，传输内容选择不应仅依赖个体重要性，而应关注传输集对输入不同方面的覆盖度。SAGE通过结合重要性过滤和多样性采样，实现了更有效的边缘-云协作推理。

Abstract: Edge-cloud hybrid inference offloads difficult inputs to a powerful remote model, but the uplink channel imposes hard per-request constraints on the number of bits that can be transmitted. We show that selecting transmitted content based solely on attention-based importance, the standard approach in collaborative inference, is inherently limited under hard budgets. Two findings support this claim. First, replacing high-importance units with low-importance but complementary ones improves server accuracy. This shows that what matters is not individual importance but how well the transmitted set covers diverse aspects of the input. Second, spatially uniform selection without any content information achieves competitive accuracy at moderate budgets. This confirms that spatial coverage alone carries independent value. Based on this analysis, we propose SAGE (Semantic Attention-Guided Evidence), a principled, training-free method that combines importance filtering with embedding-diversity sampling. SAGE achieves 93% of the server ceiling in offloaded accuracy while transmitting fewer than half of the available evidence units on ImageNet-1K, substantially outperforming importance-only composition.

</details>


### [207] [Disentangling Damage from Operational Variability: A Label-Free Self-Supervised Representation Learning Framework for Output-Only Structural Damage Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19658)
*Xudong Jian,Charikleia Stoura,Simon Scandella,Eleni Chatzi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种自监督无标签解耦表示学习框架，用于鲁棒的振动结构损伤识别，通过分离损伤相关特征与干扰变量，实现无需先验信息的端到端训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结构健康监测中的损伤识别在实际应用中常受到非损伤因素（如激励变化和环境条件）的干扰，这些因素引起的变化可能与结构损伤引起的变化相当甚至更大，从而降低损伤识别的可靠性。

Method: 采用具有两个潜在表示的自编码器直接从原始振动加速度信号学习。通过VICReg自监督不变性正则化对基线数据施加约束，同时引入频域约束确保潜在表示重建的功率谱密度与输入时间序列一致。这些机制促进表示解耦，使学习到的表示对损伤特征敏感而对干扰变量不变。

Result: 在两个真实振动数据集（桥梁和齿轮箱）上验证了框架的有效性。结果表明该方法对操作变化具有鲁棒性，具有强大的泛化能力，在损伤检测和量化方面均表现良好。

Conclusion: 该自监督无标签解耦表示学习框架能够有效分离损伤相关特征与干扰变量，无需先验损伤、激励或环境条件信息，适用于实际工程应用，为振动结构损伤识别提供了鲁棒解决方案。

Abstract: Damage identification is a core task in structural health monitoring. In practice, however, its reliability is often compromised by confounding non-damage effects, such as variations in excitation and environmental conditions, which can induce changes comparable to or larger than those caused by structural damage. To address this challenge, this study proposes a self-supervised label-free disentangled representation learning framework for robust vibration-based structural damage identification. The proposed framework employs an autoencoder with two latent representations to learn directly from raw vibration acceleration signals. A self-supervised invariance regularization, implemented via Variance-Invariance-Covariance Regularization (VICReg), is imposed on one latent representation using baseline data where structural damage is assumed constant but operational and environmental conditions vary. In addition, a frequency-domain constraint is introduced to enforce agreement between the power spectral density reconstructed from the latent representation and that computed from the corresponding input time series. Together, these mechanisms promote disentanglement, enabling the learned representation to be sensitive to damage-related characteristics while remaining invariant to nuisance variability. The framework is trained in a fully end-to-end and label-free manner, requiring no prior information on damage, excitation, or environmental conditions, making it well-suited for real-world applications. Its effectiveness is validated on two distinct real-world vibration datasets, including a bridge and a gearbox. The results demonstrate robustness to operational variability, strong generalization capability, and good performance in both damage detection and quantification.

</details>


### [208] [Budgeted Online Influence Maximization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19672)
*Pierre Perrault,Jennifer Healey,Zheng Wen,Michal Valko*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于预算约束的在线影响力最大化新框架，用总成本替代传统基数约束，更贴合实际广告场景中影响者成本差异的现实情况


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统在线影响力最大化通常采用基数约束（选择固定数量的影响者），但现实中影响者成本差异很大，广告主更关心在总预算约束下获得最佳投资回报

Method: 提出新算法，基于独立级联扩散模型和边级半赌博反馈，考虑影响者成本差异，在总预算约束下优化影响力传播

Result: 算法在预算约束下表现良好，同时该分析也适用于基数约束场景，并在此情况下改进了现有最优遗憾界

Conclusion: 预算约束框架更贴合实际广告场景，提出的算法在理论和实验上均表现优异，为在线影响力最大化提供了更实用的解决方案

Abstract: We introduce a new budgeted framework for online influence maximization, considering the total cost of an advertising campaign instead of the common cardinality constraint on a chosen influencer set. Our approach better models the real-world setting where the cost of influencers varies and advertisers want to find the best value for their overall social advertising budget. We propose an algorithm assuming an independent cascade diffusion model and edge level semi-bandit feedback, and provide both theoretical and experimental results. Our analysis is also valid for the cardinality constraint setting and improves the state of the art regret bound in this case.

</details>


### [209] [PREF-XAI: Preference-Based Personalized Rule Explanations of Black-Box Machine Learning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19684)
*Salvatore Greco,Jacek Karolczak,Roman Słowiński,Jerzy Stefanowski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于偏好的可解释人工智能(PREF-XAI)框架，将解释视为偏好驱动的决策问题，通过形式化偏好学习为用户提供个性化解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有XAI方法主要生成模型中心的解释，忽略了不同用户因目标、偏好和认知约束需要不同解释这一根本需求。虽然已有用户中心和个性化解释的研究，但大多依赖启发式调整或隐式用户建模，缺乏表示和学习个体偏好的原则性框架。

Method: 提出PREF-XAI框架，将解释重构为偏好驱动的决策问题。结合基于规则的解释和形式化偏好学习，通过用户对少量候选解释的排序来获取偏好，使用鲁棒序数回归推断加性效用函数来建模用户偏好。

Result: 在真实数据集上的实验表明，PREF-XAI能够从有限反馈中准确重建用户偏好，识别高度相关的解释，并发现用户最初未考虑的新解释规则。

Conclusion: PREF-XAI建立了XAI与偏好学习之间的连接，为交互式和自适应解释系统开辟了新方向，提供了一种原则性的个性化解释生成方法。

Abstract: Explainable artificial intelligence (XAI) has predominantly focused on generating model-centric explanations that approximate the behavior of black-box models. However, such explanations often overlook a fundamental aspect of interpretability: different users require different explanations depending on their goals, preferences, and cognitive constraints. Although recent work has explored user-centric and personalized explanations, most existing approaches rely on heuristic adaptations or implicit user modeling, lacking a principled framework for representing and learning individual preferences. In this paper, we consider Preference-Based Explainable Artificial Intelligence (PREF-XAI), a novel perspective that reframes explanation as a preference-driven decision problem. Within PREF-XAI, explanations are not treated as fixed outputs, but as alternatives to be evaluated and selected according to user-specific criteria. In the PREF-XAI perspective, here we propose a methodology that combines rule-based explanations with formal preference learning. User preferences are elicited through a ranking of a small set of candidate explanations and modeled via an additive utility function inferred using robust ordinal regression. Experimental results on real-world datasets show that PREF-XAI can accurately reconstruct user preferences from limited feedback, identify highly relevant explanations, and discover novel explanatory rules not initially considered by the user. Beyond the proposed methodology, this work establishes a connection between XAI and preference learning, opening new directions for interactive and adaptive explanation systems.

</details>


### [210] [Planning in entropy-regularized Markov decision processes and games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19695)
*Jean-Bastien Grill,Omar Darwiche Domingues,Pierre Ménard,Rémi Munos,Michal Valko*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SmoothCruiser是一种新的规划算法，用于估计熵正则化MDP和双人博弈中的值函数，在生成模型环境下实现O~(1/ε⁴)的样本复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在非正则化设置中，没有已知算法能保证最坏情况下的多项式样本复杂度。熵正则化通过促进Bellman算子的光滑性，为开发具有理论保证的高效算法提供了机会。

Method: SmoothCruiser利用熵正则化带来的Bellman算子光滑性，在环境生成模型下进行规划。算法通过正则化平滑了优化问题，使得样本复杂度与问题无关。

Result: 算法实现了O~(1/ε⁴)的样本复杂度，其中ε为期望精度。这是首个在熵正则化MDP和双人博弈中具有理论保证的多项式样本复杂度的算法。

Conclusion: 熵正则化不仅改进了策略探索，还为开发具有理论保证的高效规划算法提供了数学基础。SmoothCruiser展示了正则化在强化学习算法设计中的重要作用。

Abstract: We propose SmoothCruiser, a new planning algorithm for estimating the value function in entropy-regularized Markov decision processes and two-player games, given a generative model of the environment. SmoothCruiser makes use of the smoothness of the Bellman operator promoted by the regularization to achieve problem-independent sample complexity of order O~(1/epsilon^4) for a desired accuracy epsilon, whereas for non-regularized settings there are no known algorithms with guaranteed polynomial sample complexity in the worst case.

</details>


### [211] [On two ways to use determinantal point processes for Monte Carlo integration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19698)
*Guillaume Gautier,Rémi Bardenet,Michal Valko*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文分析基于DPP的积分估计器，比较两种方法：Bardenet & Hardy的固定DPP方法和Ermakov & Zolotukhin的定制DPP方法，并扩展到连续设置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准蒙特卡洛估计器使用独立采样，方差为O(1/N)。使用排斥性分布（DPP）可以改进估计器性能，但现有DPP方法各有局限：一种使用固定DPP但收敛速度有限，另一种需要针对特定函数定制DPP。

Method: 重新审视两种DPP估计器：1) Bardenet & Hardy的固定DPP方法，适用于光滑函数；2) Ermakov & Zolotukhin的定制DPP方法，无偏但需要针对特定函数设计DPP。将这两种方法推广到连续设置，并提供相应的采样算法。

Result: Bardenet & Hardy方法对光滑函数f的方差收敛速度为O(N^{-(1+1/d)})，优于蒙特卡洛的O(1/N)。Ermakov & Zolotukhin方法保持无偏性，方差阶数为O(1/N)，与蒙特卡洛相同，但需要针对f定制DPP。

Conclusion: DPP为积分估计提供了有前景的替代方案，在特定条件下可以超越标准蒙特卡洛方法。固定DPP方法在光滑函数上表现更好，而定制DPP方法保持无偏性但需要更多计算开销。论文为这两种方法提供了连续推广和实用采样算法。

Abstract: The standard Monte Carlo estimator $\widehat{I}_N^{\mathrm{MC}}$ of $\int fdω$ relies on independent samples from $ω$ and has variance of order $1/N$. Replacing the samples with a determinantal point process (DPP), a repulsive distribution, makes the estimator consistent, with variance rates that depend on how the DPP is adapted to $f$ and $ω$. We examine two existing DPP-based estimators: one by Bardenet & Hardy (2020) with a rate of $\mathcal{O}(N^{-(1+1/d)})$ for smooth $f$, but relying on a fixed DPP. The other, by Ermakov & Zolotukhin (1960), is unbiased with rate of order $1/N$, like Monte Carlo, but its DPP is tailored to $f$. We revisit these estimators, generalize them to continuous settings, and provide sampling algorithms.

</details>


### [212] [Ultrametric OGP - parametric RDT \emph{symmetric} binary perceptron connection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19712)
*Mihailo Stojnic*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文建立了参数化RDT与重叠间隙性质(OGPs)之间的联系，针对对称二元感知机，通过分析超度量OGPs获得约束密度上界，发现与参数化RDT估计值高度一致，并提出了两者关系的猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前研究通过fl-RDT框架表征统计计算间隙(SCGs)，在对称二元感知机中获得了算法阈值估计。本文旨在进一步连接参数化RDT与重叠间隙性质(OGPs)这一解空间的关键几何特征，探索两者之间的深层联系。

Method: 考虑s级超度量OGPs(ult_s-OGPs)，开发包含组合和概率分量的分析联合界程序。将组合部分建模为凸问题，概率部分建模为嵌套积分，进行数值评估获得约束密度上界。比较这些上界与参数化RDT在不同提升层级下的估计值。

Result: 前两个层级的最紧界：$\barα_{ult_1} \approx 1.6578$和$\barα_{ult_2} \approx 1.6219$，分别接近第3和第4提升层级的参数化RDT估计值$α_c^{(3)} \approx 1.6576$和$α_c^{(4)} \approx 1.6218$。其他关键参数（包括重叠值和超度量簇的相对大小）也表现出极好的一致性。

Conclusion: 基于观察结果，提出了连接ult-OGP和参数化RDT的猜想：算法阈值$α_a$等于两个极限的极限值，且$α_{ult_s} \leq α_{c}^{(s+2)}$（可能对某些或所有s成立）。讨论了ult-OGP和参数化RDT所有关键参数之间可能存在完全同构关系。

Abstract: In [97,99,100], an fl-RDT framework is introduced to characterize \emph{statistical computational gaps} (SCGs). Studying \emph{symmetric binary perceptrons} (SBPs), [100] obtained an \emph{algorithmic} threshold estimate $α_a\approx α_c^{(7)}\approx 1.6093$ at the 7th lifting level (for $κ=1$ margin), closely approaching $1.58$ local entropy (LE) prediction [18].
  In this paper, we further connect parametric RDT to overlap gap properties (OGPs), another key geometric feature of the solution space. Specifically, for any positive integer $s$, we consider $s$-level ultrametric OGPs ($ult_s$-OGPs) and rigorously upper-bound the associated constraint densities $α_{ult_s}$. To achieve this, we develop an analytical union-bounding program consisting of combinatorial and probabilistic components. By casting the combinatorial part as a convex problem and the probabilistic part as a nested integration, we conduct numerical evaluations and obtain that the tightest bounds at the first two levels, $\barα_{ult_1} \approx 1.6578$ and $\barα_{ult_2} \approx 1.6219$, closely approach the 3rd and 4th lifting level parametric RDT estimates, $α_c^{(3)} \approx 1.6576$ and $α_c^{(4)} \approx 1.6218$. We also observe excellent agreement across other key parameters, including overlap values and the relative sizes of ultrametric clusters.
  Based on these observations, we propose several conjectures linking $ult$-OGP and parametric RDT. Specifically, we conjecture that algorithmic threshold $α_a=\lim_{s\rightarrow\infty} α_{ult_s} = \lim_{s\rightarrow\infty} \barα{ult_s} = \lim_{r\rightarrow\infty} α_{c}^{(r)}$, and $α_{ult_s} \leq α_{c}^{(s+2)}$ (with possible equality for some (maybe even all) $s$). Finally, we discuss the potential existence of a full isomorphism connecting all key parameters of $ult$-OGP and parametric RDT.

</details>


### [213] [Adaptive MSD-Splitting: Enhancing C4.5 and Random Forests for Skewed Continuous Attributes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19722)
*Jake Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AMSD（自适应MSD分割）通过动态调整标准差乘数来改进MSD分割，解决偏态分布中的信息损失问题，并集成到随机森林中，在保持O(N)时间复杂度的同时提升准确率2-4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续数值属性的离散化是决策树归纳中的计算瓶颈。虽然MSD分割技术通过均值和标准差分箱提高了C4.5算法的效率和准确性，但其固定的一个标准差截断在高度偏态分布（如生物医学和金融数据集）中会导致灾难性的信息损失。

Method: 提出自适应MSD分割（AMSD），根据特征偏度动态调整标准差乘数，在密集区域缩小间隔以保持判别分辨率。进一步将AMSD集成到集成方法中，特别是提出了随机森林-AMSD（RF-AMSD）框架。

Result: 在Census Income、Heart Disease、Breast Cancer和Forest Covertype数据集上的实证评估表明，AMSD比标准MSD分割提高了2-4%的准确率，同时保持了与O(N log N)穷举搜索相比近乎相同的O(N)时间复杂度降低。随机森林扩展以标准计算成本的一小部分实现了最先进的准确率。

Conclusion: AMSD有效解决了MSD分割在偏态分布中的局限性，自适应统计分箱在大规模集成学习架构中具有可行性，能够在保持计算效率的同时提升模型性能。

Abstract: The discretization of continuous numerical attributes remains a persistent computational bottleneck in the induction of decision trees, particularly as dataset dimensions scale. Building upon the recently proposed MSD-Splitting technique -- which bins continuous data using the empirical mean and standard deviation to dramatically improve the efficiency and accuracy of the C4.5 algorithm -- we introduce Adaptive MSD-Splitting (AMSD). While standard MSD-Splitting is highly effective for approximately symmetric distributions, its rigid adherence to fixed one-standard-deviation cutoffs can lead to catastrophic information loss in highly skewed data, a common artifact in real-world biomedical and financial datasets. AMSD addresses this by dynamically adjusting the standard deviation multiplier based on feature skewness, narrowing intervals in dense regions to preserve discriminative resolution. Furthermore, we integrate AMSD into ensemble methods, specifically presenting the Random Forest-AMSD (RF-AMSD) framework. Empirical evaluations on the Census Income, Heart Disease, Breast Cancer, and Forest Covertype datasets demonstrate that AMSD yields a 2-4% accuracy improvement over standard MSD-Splitting, while maintaining near-identical O(N) time complexity reductions compared to the O(N log N) exhaustive search. Our Random Forest extension achieves state-of-the-art accuracy at a fraction of standard computational costs, confirming the viability of adaptive statistical binning in large-scale ensemble learning architectures.

</details>


### [214] [Benign Overfitting in Adversarial Training for Vision Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19724)
*Jiaming Zhang,Meng Ding,Shaopeng Fu,Jingfeng Zhang,Di Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 首次对简化ViT架构中对抗训练进行理论分析，证明在特定条件下ViT可实现近乎零的鲁棒训练损失和泛化误差，甚至在过拟合时仍保持强泛化能力（良性过拟合）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管ViT在各种视觉任务中取得显著成功，但与CNN类似，它们仍然容易受到对抗样本攻击。对抗训练是常见的防御策略，但其在ViT中的鲁棒性理论基础尚未得到充分探索。

Method: 对简化ViT架构进行理论分析，研究在满足特定信噪比条件和适度扰动预算下，对抗训练如何影响ViT的性能。通过合成和真实数据集实验验证理论发现。

Result: 理论分析表明，在特定条件下，对抗训练使ViT能够实现近乎零的鲁棒训练损失和鲁棒泛化误差。即使在过拟合情况下，ViT仍能保持强泛化能力，表现出"良性过拟合"现象，这一现象此前仅在CNN（带对抗训练）中被观察到。

Conclusion: 这是首次对ViT中对抗训练进行理论分析的工作，证明了在适当条件下ViT能够通过对抗训练获得鲁棒性，甚至在过拟合时仍保持良好泛化性能，为理解ViT的对抗鲁棒性提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Despite the remarkable success of Vision Transformers (ViTs) across a wide range of vision tasks, recent studies have revealed that they remain vulnerable to adversarial examples, much like Convolutional Neural Networks (CNNs). A common empirical defense strategy is adversarial training, yet the theoretical underpinnings of its robustness in ViTs remain largely unexplored. In this work, we present the first theoretical analysis of adversarial training under simplified ViT architectures. We show that, when trained under a signal-to-noise ratio that satisfies a certain condition and within a moderate perturbation budget, adversarial training enables ViTs to achieve nearly zero robust training loss and robust generalization error under certain regimes. Remarkably, this leads to strong generalization even in the presence of overfitting, a phenomenon known as \emph{benign overfitting}, previously only observed in CNNs (with adversarial training). Experiments on both synthetic and real-world datasets further validate our theoretical findings.

</details>


### [215] [FB-NLL: A Feature-Based Approach to Tackle Noisy Labels in Personalized Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19729)
*Abdulmoneam Ali,Ahmed Arafa*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FB-NLL：一种基于特征的个性化联邦学习框架，通过谱结构分析实现一次性用户聚类，并结合特征一致性策略处理噪声标签，显著提升异构数据下的个性化性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于学习动态的PFL方法容易受到低质量数据和噪声标签的影响，因为损坏的更新会扭曲聚类决策并降低个性化性能。需要一种能够解耦用户聚类与迭代训练动态、对噪声鲁棒的方法。

Method: FB-NLL采用特征中心框架：1）通过特征表示协方差的谱结构表征用户，利用子空间相似性一次性识别任务一致的用户分组；2）基于特征一致性的检测和校正策略，利用学习特征空间中的方向对齐和类特定特征子空间分配标签，缓解噪声监督。

Result: 在多种数据集和噪声机制下的广泛实验表明，FB-NLL在平均准确率和性能稳定性方面持续优于现有最先进的基线方法，同时显著减少了通信开销和计算成本。

Conclusion: FB-NLL通过解耦用户聚类与训练动态、利用特征空间几何结构实现一次性聚类，并结合特征一致性处理噪声标签，为异构和噪声环境下的个性化联邦学习提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Personalized Federated Learning (PFL) aims to learn multiple task-specific models rather than a single global model across heterogeneous data distributions. Existing PFL approaches typically rely on iterative optimization-such as model update trajectories-to cluster users that need to accomplish the same tasks together. However, these learning-dynamics-based methods are inherently vulnerable to low-quality data and noisy labels, as corrupted updates distort clustering decisions and degrade personalization performance. To tackle this, we propose FB-NLL, a feature-centric framework that decouples user clustering from iterative training dynamics. By exploiting the intrinsic heterogeneity of local feature spaces, FB-NLL characterizes each user through the spectral structure of the covariances of their feature representations and leverages subspace similarity to identify task-consistent user groupings. This geometry-aware clustering is label-agnostic and is performed in a one-shot manner prior to training, significantly reducing communication overhead and computational costs compared to iterative baselines.
  Complementing this, we introduce a feature-consistency-based detection and correction strategy to address noisy labels within clusters. By leveraging directional alignment in the learned feature space and assigning labels based on class-specific feature subspaces, our method mitigates corrupted supervision without requiring estimation of stochastic noise transition matrices. In addition, FB-NLL is model-independent and integrates seamlessly with existing noise-robust training techniques. Extensive experiments across diverse datasets and noise regimes demonstrate that our framework consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines in terms of average accuracy and performance stability.

</details>


### [216] [FASTER: Value-Guided Sampling for Fast RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19730)
*Perry Dong,Alexander Swerdlow,Dorsa Sadigh,Chelsea Finn*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FASTER是一种轻量级方法，通过在去噪过程中早期预测动作候选的下游价值来筛选动作，避免了基于采样的测试时扩展的计算成本，可提升现有生成式RL算法的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前性能最好的强化学习算法通常使用测试时扩展方法（如采样多个动作候选并选择最佳），但这些方法计算成本高昂。需要一种方法能在不增加计算负担的情况下获得基于采样的测试时扩展的好处。

Method: 将基于扩散策略的多动作候选去噪和选择过程建模为马尔可夫决策过程（MDP），目标是在去噪完成前逐步筛选动作候选。学习去噪空间中的策略和价值函数，预测动作候选在去噪过程中的下游价值，并在最大化回报的同时进行筛选。

Result: 在具有挑战性的长时程操作任务（在线和批量在线RL）中，FASTER持续改进基础策略，并在对比方法中取得最佳整体性能。应用于预训练的VLA时，FASTER在显著降低训练和推理计算需求的同时达到相同性能。

Conclusion: FASTER提供了一种轻量级方法，可在不增加计算成本的情况下获得基于采样的测试时扩展的好处，可轻松集成到现有生成式RL算法中，在保持性能的同时大幅降低计算需求。

Abstract: Some of the most performant reinforcement learning algorithms today can be prohibitively expensive as they use test-time scaling methods such as sampling multiple action candidates and selecting the best one. In this work, we propose FASTER, a method for getting the benefits of sampling-based test-time scaling of diffusion-based policies without the computational cost by tracing the performance gain of action samples back to earlier in the denoising process. Our key insight is that we can model the denoising of multiple action candidates and selecting the best one as a Markov Decision Process (MDP) where the goal is to progressively filter action candidates before denoising is complete. With this MDP, we can learn a policy and value function in the denoising space that predicts the downstream value of action candidates in the denoising process and filters them while maximizing returns. The result is a method that is lightweight and can be plugged into existing generative RL algorithms. Across challenging long-horizon manipulation tasks in online and batch-online RL, FASTER consistently improves the underlying policies and achieves the best overall performance among the compared methods. Applied to a pretrained VLA, FASTER achieves the same performance while substantially reducing training and inference compute requirements. Code is available at https://github.com/alexanderswerdlow/faster .

</details>


### [217] [Safe Continual Reinforcement Learning in Non-stationary Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19737)
*Austin Coursey,Abel Diaz-Gonzalez,Marcos Quinones-Grueiro,Gautam Biswas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文系统研究了安全持续强化学习，通过创建三个基准环境评估现有方法，揭示了安全约束与防止灾难性遗忘之间的基本张力，并探索了正则化策略来缓解这一矛盾。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统强化学习控制器假设环境平稳，但在实际非平稳部署中，系统动态和操作条件会意外变化。同时，物理环境中的RL控制器必须在学习和执行阶段都满足安全约束。虽然持续RL和安全RL分别解决了非平稳性和安全性问题，但二者的交叉领域仍相对未被探索，因此需要研究能够在系统生命周期内适应变化同时保持安全的安全持续RL算法。

Method: 通过引入三个捕捉安全关键持续适应的基准环境，系统评估了来自安全RL、持续RL及其组合的代表性方法。研究了正则化策略来缓解安全约束与防止灾难性遗忘之间的权衡，并分析了这些策略的优缺点。

Result: 实证结果表明，在非平稳动态下，维持安全约束与防止灾难性遗忘之间存在基本张力，现有方法通常无法同时实现这两个目标。正则化策略能够部分缓解这一权衡，但仍存在局限性。

Conclusion: 需要开发能够在变化环境中持续自主操作的安全、弹性的基于学习的控制器。论文概述了关键开放挑战和研究方向，为安全持续RL的未来发展提供了路线图。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) offers a compelling data-driven paradigm for synthesizing controllers for complex systems when accurate physical models are unavailable; however, most existing control-oriented RL methods assume stationarity and, therefore, struggle in real-world non-stationary deployments where system dynamics and operating conditions can change unexpectedly. Moreover, RL controllers acting in physical environments must satisfy safety constraints throughout their learning and execution phases, rendering transient violations during adaptation unacceptable. Although continual RL and safe RL have each addressed non-stationarity and safety, respectively, their intersection remains comparatively unexplored, motivating the study of safe continual RL algorithms that can adapt over the system's lifetime while preserving safety. In this work, we systematically investigate safe continual reinforcement learning by introducing three benchmark environments that capture safety-critical continual adaptation and by evaluating representative approaches from safe RL, continual RL, and their combinations. Our empirical results reveal a fundamental tension between maintaining safety constraints and preventing catastrophic forgetting under non-stationary dynamics, with existing methods generally failing to achieve both objectives simultaneously. To address this shortcoming, we examine regularization-based strategies that partially mitigate this trade-off and characterize their benefits and limitations. Finally, we outline key open challenges and research directions toward developing safe, resilient learning-based controllers capable of sustained autonomous operation in changing environments.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [218] [Dual-View Training for Instruction-Following Information Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18845)
*Qingcheng Zeng,Puxuan Yu,Aman Mehta,Fuheng Zhao,Rajhans Samdani*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出基于极性反转的双视图数据合成策略，通过生成互补指令使同一文档对在不同指令下交换相关性标签，从而训练检索器更好地遵循用户指令约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有检索系统主要针对语义相关性训练，难以区分符合查询主题的文档与满足具体指令约束的文档，导致在指令跟随信息检索任务中表现不佳。

Method: 基于极性反转的双视图数据合成策略：给定查询、符合指令的相关文档和违反指令的硬负例，使用LLM生成互补指令使两个文档交换相关性标签，通过同一候选集在不同指令下的不同标签训练检索器关注指令而非固定主题线索。

Result: 在305M参数的编码器上，该方法在FollowIR基准测试中性能提升45%，超越规模相当或更大的通用嵌入模型。在匹配数据预算下，数据多样性和指令监督具有互补作用：前者保持通用检索质量，后者提高指令敏感性。

Conclusion: 针对性数据合成对于构建既具备广泛能力又具有指令感知的检索系统具有重要价值，双视图训练策略能有效提升检索器对用户指令约束的理解和遵循能力。

Abstract: Instruction-following information retrieval (IF-IR) studies retrieval systems that must not only find documents relevant to a query, but also obey explicit user constraints such as required attributes, exclusions, or output preferences. However, most retrievers are trained primarily for semantic relevance and often fail to distinguish documents that match the topic from those that satisfy the instruction. We propose a dual-view data synthesis strategy based on polarity reversal: given a query, a document that is relevant under the instruction, and a hard negative that matches the query but violates the instruction, we prompt an LLM to generate a complementary instruction under which the two documents swap relevance labels. By presenting the same document pair under complementary instructions that invert their relevance labels, the training signal forces the retriever to reconsider the same candidate set through the instruction, rather than relying on fixed topical cues. On a 305M-parameter encoder, our method improves performance on the FollowIR benchmark by 45%, surpassing general-purpose embedding models of comparable or larger scale. Through head-to-head comparisons at matched data budgets, we further show that data diversity and instruction supervision play complementary roles: the former preserves general retrieval quality, while the latter improves instruction sensitivity. These results highlight the value of targeted data synthesis for building retrieval systems that are both broadly capable and instruction-aware.

</details>


### [219] [STK-Adapter: Incorporating Evolving Graph and Event Chain for Temporal Knowledge Graph Extrapolation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19042)
*Shuyuan Zhao,Wei Chen,Weijie Zhang,Xinrui Hou,Junfeng Shen,Boyan Shi,Shengnan Guo,Youfang Lin,Huaiyu Wan*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: STK-Adapter：一种时空知识适配器，通过多专家机制解决TKG外推中图结构表示与LLM语义空间对齐不足的问题，显著提升预测性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在将TKG的演化图结构表示和文本事件链集成到LLM时面临两个主要挑战：1）由于图结构表示与LLM语义空间的浅层对齐导致时空信息丢失；2）在LLM微调过程中TKG演化结构特征逐渐稀释

Method: 提出STK-Adapter，包含三个MoE模块：时空MoE捕获TKG的空间结构和时间模式；事件感知MoE建模事件链中的复杂时序语义依赖；跨模态对齐MoE通过TKG引导的注意力专家促进深度跨模态对齐

Result: 在基准数据集上的大量实验表明，STK-Adapter显著优于最先进方法，并在跨数据集任务中展现出强大的泛化能力

Conclusion: STK-Adapter通过灵活的图编码器与LLM集成，有效解决了TKG外推中的时空信息对齐问题，为TKG推理提供了有效的解决方案

Abstract: Temporal Knowledge Graph (TKG) extrapolation aims to predict future events based on historical facts. Recent studies have attempted to enhance TKG extrapolation by integrating TKG's evolving structural representations and textual event chains into Large Language Models (LLMs). Yet, two main challenges limit these approaches: (1) The loss of essential spatial-temporal information due to shallow alignment between TKG's graph evolving structural representation and the LLM's semantic space, and (2) the progressive dilution of the TKG's evolving structural features during LLM fine-tuning. To address these challenges, we propose the Spatial-Temporal Knowledge Adapter (STK-Adapter), which flexibly integrates the evolving graph encoder and the LLM to facilitate TKG reasoning. In STK-Adapter, a Spatial-Temporal MoE is designed to capture spatial structures and temporal patterns inherent in TKGs. An Event-Aware MoE is employed to model intricate temporal semantics dependencies within event chains. In addition, a Cross-Modality Alignment MoE is proposed to facilitate deep cross-modality alignment by TKG-guided attention experts. Extensive experiments on benchmark datasets demonstrate that STK-Adapter significantly outperforms state-of-the-art methods and exhibits strong generalization capabilities in cross-dataset task. The code is available at https://github.com/Zhaoshuyuan0246/STK-Adapter.

</details>


### [220] [Think Before Writing: Feature-Level Multi-Objective Optimization for Generative Citation Visibility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19113)
*Zikang Liu,Peilan Xu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: FeatGEO是一个特征级的多目标优化框架，通过将网页抽象为可解释的结构、内容和语言特征来优化生成式引擎中的引用可见性，同时保持内容质量，优于现有的标记级文本重写方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式答案引擎通过选择性引用而非排名检索来呈现内容，这从根本上改变了可见性的确定方式。现有生成式引擎优化方法主要依赖标记级文本重写，可解释性有限且对引用可见性和内容质量之间的权衡控制较弱。

Method: FeatGEO是一个特征级、多目标优化框架，将网页抽象为可解释的结构、内容和语言属性。它不直接编辑文本，而是在特征空间中进行优化，并使用语言模型将特征配置实现为自然语言，从而将高级优化与表层生成解耦。

Result: 在GEO-Bench基准测试中，针对三个生成式引擎的实验表明，FeatGEO持续提高引用可见性，同时保持或改善内容质量，显著优于标记级基线方法。进一步分析显示，引用行为更受文档级内容属性的影响而非孤立的词汇编辑，且学习的特征配置在不同规模的语言模型间具有泛化性。

Conclusion: FeatGEO通过特征级优化框架有效解决了生成式引擎优化中的引用可见性与内容质量权衡问题，提供了比传统标记级方法更好的可解释性和控制能力，为生成式引擎优化开辟了新方向。

Abstract: Generative answer engines expose content through selective citation rather than ranked retrieval, fundamentally altering how visibility is determined. This shift calls for new optimization methods beyond traditional search engine optimization. Existing generative engine optimization (GEO) approaches primarily rely on token-level text rewriting, offering limited interpretability and weak control over the trade-off between citation visibility and content quality. We propose FeatGEO, a feature-level, multi-objective optimization framework that abstracts webpages into interpretable structural, content, and linguistic properties. Instead of directly editing text, FeatGEO optimizes over this feature space and uses a language model to realize feature configurations into natural language, decoupling high-level optimization from surface-level generation. Experiments on GEO-Bench across three generative engines demonstrate that FeatGEO consistently improves citation visibility while maintaining or improving content quality, substantially outperforming token-level baselines. Further analyses show that citation behavior is more strongly influenced by document-level content properties than by isolated lexical edits, and that the learned feature configurations generalize across language models of different scales.

</details>


### [221] [GraphRAG-IRL: Personalized Recommendation with Graph-Grounded Inverse Reinforcement Learning and LLM Re-ranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19128)
*Siqi Liang,Xiawei Wang,Yudi Zhang,Jiaying Zhou*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: GraphRAG-IRL：结合图检索增强生成、逆强化学习和个性化LLM重排的混合推荐框架，通过图结构特征构建、IRL校准预排序和LLM语义重排提升推荐性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于大语言模型的推荐系统存在校准性差、对候选顺序敏感、流行度偏差等问题，使其作为独立排序引擎不可靠。需要结合LLM的语义推理能力和更稳定的排序方法

Method: 构建包含物品、类别和概念的异质知识图谱，检索个体和群体偏好上下文；使用最大熵逆强化学习模型进行校准预排序；对短候选列表应用基于人物角色的LLM提示进行语义判断，并与IRL排序结果融合

Result: GraphRAG-IRL作为独立推荐器表现优异：IRL-MLP结合GraphRAG在MovieLens上NDCG@10提升15.7%，在KuaiRand上提升16.6%；IRL和GraphRAG具有超加性效应；人物角色引导的LLM融合进一步改善排序质量，在MovieLens ml-1m上相比IRL基线提升16.8%

Conclusion: GraphRAG-IRL框架通过结合图结构特征、逆强化学习和LLM语义推理，解决了纯提示排序的局限性，实现了校准、稳定且语义丰富的推荐系统

Abstract: Personalized recommendation requires models that capture sequential user preferences while remaining robust to sparse feedback and semantic ambiguity. Recent work has explored large language models (LLMs) as recommenders and re-rankers, but pure prompt-based ranking often suffers from poor calibration, sensitivity to candidate ordering, and popularity bias. These limitations make LLMs useful semantic reasoners, but unreliable as standalone ranking engines.
  We present \textbf{GraphRAG-IRL}, a hybrid recommendation framework that combines graph-grounded feature construction, inverse reinforcement learning (IRL), and persona-guided LLM re-ranking. Our method constructs a heterogeneous knowledge graph over items, categories, and concepts, retrieves both individual and community preference context, and uses these signals to train a Maximum Entropy IRL model for calibrated pre-ranking. An LLM is then applied only to a short candidate list, where persona-guided prompts provide complementary semantic judgments that are fused with IRL rankings.
  Experiments show that GraphRAG-IRL is a strong standalone recommender: IRL-MLP with GraphRAG improves NDCG@10 by 15.7\% on MovieLens and 16.6\% on KuaiRand over supervised baselines. The results also show that IRL and GraphRAG are superadditive, with the combined gain exceeding the sum of their individual improvements. Persona-guided LLM fusion further improves ranking quality, yielding up to 16.8\% NDCG@10 improvement over the IRL-only baseline on MovieLens ml-1m, while score fusion on KuaiRand provides consistent gains of 4--6\% across LLM providers.

</details>


### [222] [CAST: Modeling Semantic-Level Transitions for Complementary-Aware Sequential Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19414)
*Qian Zhang,Lech Szymanski,Haibo Zhang,Jeremiah D. Deng*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: CAST框架通过语义级转移建模和LLM验证的互补先验注入，有效捕捉细粒度语义依赖，解决传统序列推荐中基于稀疏共购统计的伪相关性问题，显著提升推荐性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统序列推荐模型依赖稀疏共购统计，容易将流行度偏差等伪相关误认为真实互补关系；现有语义方法将离散语义编码聚合为粗粒度物品表示，模糊了识别互补性所需的特定语义细节。

Method: 提出互补感知语义转移(CAST)框架：1) 语义级转移模块直接在离散语义编码空间建模动态转移，捕捉细粒度语义依赖；2) 互补先验注入模块将LLM验证的互补先验整合到注意力机制中，优先考虑互补模式而非共现统计。

Result: 在多个电商数据集上，CAST持续优于最先进方法，Recall提升达17.6%，NDCG提升达16.0%，训练加速65倍，验证了其在超越统计层面挖掘潜在物品互补性的有效性和效率。

Conclusion: CAST通过语义级转移建模和LLM验证的互补先验，有效解决了序列推荐中基于统计的伪相关问题，能够更准确地捕捉物品间的真实互补关系，显著提升推荐性能。

Abstract: Sequential Recommendation (SR) aims to predict the next interaction of a user based on their behavior sequence, where complementary relations often provide essential signals for predicting the next item. However, mainstream models relying on sparse co-purchase statistics often mistake spurious correlations (e.g., due to popularity bias) for true complementary relations. Identifying true complementary relations requires capturing the fine-grained item semantics (e.g., specifications) that simple cooccurrence statistics would be unable to model. While recent semantics-based methods utilize discrete semantic codes to represent items, they typically aggregate semantic codes into coarse item representations. This aggregation process blurs specific semantic details required to identify complementarity. To address these critical limitations and effectively leverage semantics for capturing reliable complementary relations, we propose a Complementary-Aware Semantic Transition (CAST) framework that introduces a new modeling paradigm built upon semantic-level transitions. Specifically, a semantic-level transition module is designed to model dynamic transitions directly in the discrete semantic code space, effectively capturing fine-grained semantic dependencies often lost in aggregated item representations. Then, a complementary prior injection module is designed to incorporate LLM-verified complementary priors into the attention mechanism, thereby prioritizing complementary patterns over co-occurrence statistics. Experiments on multiple e-commerce datasets demonstrate that CAST consistently outperforms the state-of-the-art approaches, achieving up to 17.6% Recall and 16.0% NDCG gains with 65x training acceleration. This validates its effectiveness and efficiency in uncovering latent item complementarity beyond statistics. The code will be released upon acceptance.

</details>


### [223] [Enhancing Unsupervised Keyword Extraction in Academic Papers through Integrating Highlights with Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19505)
*Yi Xiang,Chengzhi Zhang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 该论文研究如何利用学术论文的highlights部分提升无监督关键词提取性能，发现结合abstract和highlights能显著改善提取效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要关注abstract和references进行关键词提取，但highlights部分包含有价值的摘要信息，能有效补充abstract，但尚未被充分探索。

Method: 评估三种输入场景：仅使用abstract、仅使用highlights、以及两者结合。在计算机科学和图书馆信息科学数据集上使用四种无监督模型进行实验。

Result: 实验表明，将abstract与highlights结合能显著提升关键词提取性能。同时分析了abstract和highlights在关键词覆盖和内容上的差异及其对提取结果的影响。

Conclusion: highlights部分包含有价值的关键词信息，将其与abstract结合能有效提升无监督关键词提取性能，为学术关键词提取提供了新的视角和方法。

Abstract: Automatic keyword extraction from academic papers is a key area of interest in natural language processing and information retrieval. Although previous research has mainly focused on utilizing abstract and references for keyword extraction, this paper focuses on the highlights section - a summary describing the key findings and contributions, offering readers a quick overview of the research. Our observations indicate that highlights contain valuable keyword information that can effectively complement the abstract. To investigate the impact of incorporating highlights into unsupervised keyword extraction, we evaluate three input scenarios: using only the abstract, the highlights, and a combination of both. Experiments conducted with four unsupervised models on Computer Science (CS), Library and Information Science (LIS) datasets reveal that integrating the abstract with highlights significantly improves extraction performance. Furthermore, we examine the differences in keyword coverage and content between abstract and highlights, exploring how these variations influence extraction outcomes. The data and code are available at https://github.com/xiangyi-njust/Highlight-KPE.

</details>


### [224] [LoopCTR: Unlocking the Loop Scaling Power for Click-Through Rate Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19550)
*Jiakai Tang,Runfeng Zhang,Weiqiu Wang,Yifei Liu,Chuan Wang,Xu Chen,Yeqiu Yang,Jian Wu,Yuning Jiang,Bo Zheng*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: LoopCTR：一种通过循环缩放范式解耦计算与参数增长的Transformer CTR模型，采用三明治架构和过程监督，实现训练多循环、推理零循环的高效策略


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统Transformer CTR模型通过堆叠更多参数进行扩展时，会带来计算和存储开销的急剧增长，导致扩展雄心与工业部署的严格约束之间存在日益扩大的差距

Method: 提出LoopCTR循环缩放范式，通过递归重用共享模型层来增加训练时计算，解耦计算与参数增长。采用增强的三明治架构（包含超连接残差和专家混合），并在每个循环深度使用过程监督将多循环优势编码到共享参数中
